keep thinking I ruined everything for my family and the parish, and sometimes I feel like even the little things that went wrong are my punishment.\n- key: S033\n  name: Elevated_Irritable_Mood\n But... yes. \n\nA few nights... I heard my mother. She's passed on, but... she was telling me where to put the marigolds and the candles. My husband said he didn't hear anything. And... one time, I saw her, too. Just standing by the kitchen doorway. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Can you explain to me in a step-by-step way how you think the altar and parish committee are being used against you?\n\nPatient: I don't... I can't really give you a list. There's not much to tell.\n\nDoctor: Can you describe your thought process when you're thinking about your in-laws using the altar against you?\n\nPatient: Look, I don't really have a... a thought process about it. Now they're actually using the altar, and getting the parish committee involved, and... it's all just to push me out of the picture. They want to make sure everyone forgets everything I've done for this family. \n\nThere isn't a process to describe. It's just what is happening. I don't know why you keep asking me to break it down.\n\nDoctor: Do you often find it difficult to express your thoughts in a logical and coherent way?\n\nPatient: No... I don't think so. I mean, I'm talking to you right now, aren't I? I just... I don't always want to talk. It's just... sometimes it's hard to explain it to someone else, especially when they keep asking so many questions. I just... I think about what they're doing. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you jump from one idea to another when talking about your in-laws?\n\nPatient: No... I don't do that. I just stick to what I'm saying about them. There's no jumping around.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like your thoughts are disconnected from each other when you're thinking about your situation?\n\nPatient: No... I don't. They aren't disconnected. Just trying to push me out... to make everyone forget what I've done for this family.\n\nDoctor: Do you really believe your in-laws are using the altar and parish committee against you?\n\nPatient: Yes. I know they are. It's... it's not just a feeling. They're using the church... the altar... to turn everyone against me. To erase everything I've done.\n\nDoctor: Have you ever heard voices or sounds that other people cannot hear?\n\nPatient: I... well. I don't know if it's what you mean. But... yes. \n\nA few nights... I heard my mother. She's passed on, but... she was telling me where to put the marigolds and the candles. My husband said he didn't hear anything. And... one time, I saw her, too. Just standing by the kitchen doorway. He told me no one was there... but I know she was.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about what you mean by 'erase everything I've done'?\n\nPatient: Well... it's just what I said. They don't want anyone to remember. I raised the children... I kept the house together. But now... they want it all gone. So they use the altar. And the committee. Just... plotting to make sure I don't matter anymore. That's all.\n\nDoctor: Can you explain to me in a step-by-step way how you think the altar and parish committee are being used against you?\n\nPatient: I don't... I can't really give you a list. It's just what I know. \n\nI came in here because... well, I knew my in-laws weren't just arguing with me anymore. It's more than that. They use the altar... and they talk to the parish committee... just to push me out. To make everyone forget everything I've done for this family. \n\nI don't really know how else to explain it. That's just what they're doing.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about what you mean by 'they use the altar'?\n\nPatient: I just... I already told you. They use it. My in-laws, they... they go there and they use it to turn the committee against me. I don't know how else to say it. They just want me gone, and they want everything I've done erased. That's it.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me something that happened to you recently, and how it made you feel, in as much detail as you can?\n\nPatient: I don't really know what you want me to say. I just... I sit at home mostly. I was making tea the other day, and... I just felt tired. That's all. There's not much to tell.\n\nDoctor: Can you describe your thought process when you're thinking about your in-laws using the altar against you?\n\nPatient: Look, I don't really have a... a thought process about it. Now they're actually using the altar, and getting the parish committee involved, and... it's all just to push me out of the picture.
